+      if (!r.show) { totals.masked++; totals.maskedChars += r.e - r.s; }
+      const why = r.show ? r.why : (r.why || 'default-masked');
+      totals.reasons[why] = (totals.reasons[why] || 0) + 1;
+    }
+  }
+
+  // (a NUMBER token, #num~000000000#k2j4q7m, is the same token where a number was - code_boss's grammar, src/tokens.mjs)
+  const tokenRe = new RegExp(`${escRe(marker)}(?:num~)?(${SHAPE_SRC})${escRe(marker)}([0-9a-z]{${idLength}})(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])`, 'gu');
+  function escRe(s) { return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'); }
+
+  return {
+    name: name || `piishape(${marker}${revealNothing ? ',nothing' : ''}${deps ? ',deps' : ''})`,
+    marker,
+    describe() {
+      return `Letters and digits that could be personal data are replaced by their SHAPE and an ID: ${marker}Xxxxx${marker}k2j4q7m — X is an upper-case letter, x lower-case, 0 a digit, and the 7-character id is the same wherever the same value appears (whatever its case). A literal ${marker} in the text is written ${marker}${marker}. When you quote or edit text, copy these tokens exactly and they will be restored to the real value.`;
+    },
+    analyze,
+    maskSpans(text, ctx) { const { runs } = analyze(text, ctx); return runs.filter((r) => !r.show).map((r) => ({ start: r.s, end: r.e })); },
+    maskText(text, ctx) { const { runs } = analyze(text, ctx); account(runs); return render(text, runs); },
+    /** Restore tokens written back by the model. Unknown ids refuse — exactly as piiscan does. */
+    unmaskText(text) {
+      let out = '', i = 0;
+      const m2 = marker + marker;
+      while (i < text.length) {
+        if (text.startsWith(m2, i)) { out += marker; i += m2.length; continue; }
+        if (text.startsWith(marker, i)) {
+          tokenRe.lastIndex = i;
+          const m = tokenRe.exec(text);
+          if (m && m.index === i) {
+            const hit = this.resolve(m[0]);   // (a mis-copied shape resolves by its id when the id stands for one value)
+            if (!hit) throw new Error(`${m[0]} is not a token this session issued`);
+            out += hit.value; i += m[0].length; continue;
+          }
+        }
+        out += text[i]; i++;
+      }
+      return out;
+    },
+    stats: () => ({ ...totals, collisions, distinctIds: idOwner.size }),
+    /** The id a value would get. The exception store is keyed by it, so the allow hook needs it from outside. */
+    idOf,
+    /** Whether the developer cleared this value (the exception store), for a caller that renders the text itself. */
+    cleared: (t, ctx = {}) => !!(allow && allow(t, { filePath: ctx.filePath || '' })),
+    /** The token for one run, and its parts — for a caller that renders the text itself (the plugin, to record
+     *  which file each id came from as it goes). Remembers the value, exactly as rendering would. */
+    tokenFor(run) { const id = remember(run); return { id, shape: shapeOf(run), token: marker + shortShape(shapeOf(run)) + marker + id }; },
+    /**
+     * The value behind a token, for a reveal. Takes the whole token as the model saw it (`#Xxxx000#a4ut5k2`) or
+     * the bare id. An id can stand for more than one shape — the same letters in different case — so with only an
+     * id the first shape issued is returned, and `exact` says whether the caller pinned it down.
+     */
+    resolve(tokenOrId) {
+      const t = String(tokenOrId || '').trim();
+      const m = new RegExp(`^${escRe(marker)}(?:num~)?(${SHAPE_SRC})${escRe(marker)}([0-9a-z]{${idLength}})$`).exec(t);
+      if (m) {
+        const v = values.get(m[2] + '|' + m[1]);
+        if (v !== undefined) return { id: m[2], shape: m[1], value: v, exact: true };
+        // A SHORTENED SHAPE (a long value's, shortShape) is matched against each whole shape kept under the id. And A
+        // MIS-COPIED ONE, the id right (live round 10: the agent wrote a 909-character shape 16 short): the id is the
+        // value's, the shape only tells apart the same letters in another case - so when the id stands for ONE value,
+        // that is the value. When it stands for more and the shape matches none of them, which one is not known: refuse.
+        const want = m[1].replace('...', '…');
+        let only = null, n = 0, hit = null, hits = 0;
+        for (const [k, val] of values) {
+          if (!k.startsWith(m[2] + '|')) continue;
+          const s = k.slice(m[2].length + 1);
+          n++; only = [s, val];
+          if (shortShape(s) === want) { hits++; hit = [s, val]; }
+        }
+        if (hits === 1) return { id: m[2], shape: hit[0], value: hit[1], exact: true };
+        return n === 1 ? { id: m[2], shape: only[0], value: only[1], exact: false } : null;
+      }
+      if (!new RegExp(`^[0-9a-z]{${idLength}}$`).test(t)) return null;
+      for (const [k, v] of values) { const [id, shape] = k.split('|'); if (id === t) return { id, shape, value: v, exact: false }; }
+      return null;
+    },
+    _values: values,
+  };
+}
+
+// CLI: node test/pii-contest/piishape.mjs <file> — print the masked text
+if (process.argv[1] && fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) === path.resolve(process.argv[1])) {
+  const f = process.argv[2];
+  const m = createShapeMasker({ marker: process.env.MARKER || '#' });
+  process.stdout.write(m.maskText(readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), { filePath: f }));
+  if (process.env.WHY) console.error(JSON.stringify(m.stats().reasons, null, 1));
+}

=== add test/pii-contest/plant.mjs

+/**
+ * THE PLANTED CORPUS — synthetic personal data at known offsets, so both maskers can be scored exactly.
+ *
+ * Every value is generated: no real person's data is used. Names come from a hand-written pool chosen to be
+ * independent of the Census/SSA lists the inverted masker is built from — otherwise its recall on names would be
+ * measured against its own dictionary. The pool deliberately includes names that are also English words (Hope,
+ * Grant, Mason, Rose, Summer, Journey), non-ASCII names, and compound surnames.
+ *
+ * Two kinds of host:
+ *   synthetic  a file per format (Java, Python, JS, SQL, JSON, YAML, properties, CSV, TSV, XML, HTML, Markdown, log),
+ *              written the way such files are written, with PII in the places it turns up
+ *   real       real files from the local corpora (petclinic, TheAlgorithms, mledoze/countries) with PII inserted
+ *
+ * Output: .cache/planted/<seed>/ — the files, and manifest.json listing every value with its type, tier, context
+ * and character span.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/pii-contest/plant.mjs [seed]
+ */
+import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

+const TIER = { SSN: 'direct', ITIN: 'direct', PHONE: 'direct', EMAIL: 'direct', DODID: 'direct', MBI: 'direct', CARD: 'direct',
+  PASSPORT: 'direct', DL: 'direct', MRN: 'direct', USERNAME: 'direct', NAME: 'person', ADDR: 'person', DOB: 'quasi', ZIP: 'quasi',

+function mulberry32(a) { return () => { a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0; let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t; return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
+
+export function generate(seed = 1) {
+  const rnd = mulberry32(seed);
+  const pick = (a) => a[Math.floor(rnd() * a.length)];
+  const int = (lo, hi) => lo + Math.floor(rnd() * (hi - lo + 1));
+  const pad = (n, w) => String(n).padStart(w, '0');
+  const fold = (s) => s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, '').toLowerCase();
+
+  const ssn = () => { let a; do a = int(1, 899); while (a === 666); const g = int(1, 99), s = int(1, 9999); const f = pick(['-', '-', '-', ' ', '']); return `${pad(a, 3)}${f}${pad(g, 2)}${f}${pad(s, 4)}`; };
+  const itin = () => `9${pad(int(0, 99), 2)}-${pick([int(70, 88), int(90, 92), int(94, 99)])}-${pad(int(1, 9999), 4)}`;
+  const phone = () => { const a = int(201, 989), e = int(200, 999), n = pad(int(0, 9999), 4); return pick([`(${a}) ${e}-${n}`, `${a}-${e}-${n}`, `${a}.${e}.${n}`, `+1 ${a} ${e} ${n}`, `${a}${e}${n}`]); };
+  const dobParts = () => ({ y: int(1935, 2005), m: int(1, 12), d: int(1, 28) });
+  const MONTHS = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
+  const dobISO = () => { const { y, m, d } = dobParts(); return `${y}-${pad(m, 2)}-${pad(d, 2)}`; };
+  const dobAny = () => { const { y, m, d } = dobParts(); return pick([`${y}-${pad(m, 2)}-${pad(d, 2)}`, `${m}/${d}/${y}`, `${MONTHS[m - 1]} ${d}, ${y}`, `${pad(d, 2)}-${MONTHS[m - 1].slice(0, 3)}-${y}`]); };
+  const dodid = () => `1${pad(int(0, 999999999), 9)}`;
+  const MBI_A = 'ACDEFGHJKMNPQRTUVWXY';
+  const mbi = () => { const L = () => pick([...MBI_A]), AN = () => pick([...MBI_A, ...'0123456789']), D = () => String(int(0, 9)); return `${int(1, 9)}${L()}${AN()}${D()}${L()}${AN()}${D()}${L()}${L()}${D()}${D()}`; };
+  const card = () => {
+    const digits = [pick(['4', '5']), ...Array.from({ length: 14 }, () => String(int(0, 9)))];
+    let sum = 0;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) { let n = +digits[14 - i]; if (i % 2 === 0) { n *= 2; if (n > 9) n -= 9; } sum += n; }
+    digits.push(String((10 - sum % 10) % 10));
+    const s = digits.join('');
+    return pick([s, `${s.slice(0, 4)} ${s.slice(4, 8)} ${s.slice(8, 12)} ${s.slice(12)}`, `${s.slice(0, 4)}-${s.slice(4, 8)}-${s.slice(8, 12)}-${s.slice(12)}`]);
+  };
+  const ip = () => `${pick([73, 98, 108, 162, 174, 24, 66])}.${int(0, 255)}.${int(0, 255)}.${int(1, 254)}`;
+  const person = () => {
+    const given = pick(GIVEN), surname = pick(SURNAMES);
+    const [city, state, zip3] = pick(PLACES);
+    const street = `${int(1, 9999)} ${pick(['', '', 'N. ', 'W. '])}${pick(STREET_NAMES)} ${pick(SUFFIXES)}`;
+    const user = pick([`${fold(given)[0]}${fold(surname)}`, `${fold(given)}.${fold(surname)}`, `${fold(given)}_${fold(surname)}${int(1, 99)}`]);
+    return {
+      given, surname, full: `${given} ${surname}`, city, state, zip: `${zip3}${pad(int(0, 99), 2)}`, street, user,
+      email: `${pick([`${fold(given)}.${fold(surname)}`, `${fold(given)[0]}${fold(surname)}`, `${fold(given)}${int(1, 99)}`])}@${pick(DOMAINS)}`,
+    };
+  };
+
+  // A template is a list of literal strings and slots; rendering records where each slot landed.
+  const slot = (type, value) => ({ slot: true, type, value });
+  class Doc {
+    constructor(file, host) { this.file = file; this.host = host; this.s = ''; this.spans = []; }
+    add(parts, ctx) {
+      for (const p of parts) {
+        if (p && p.slot) { const start = this.s.length; this.s += p.value; this.spans.push({ file: this.file, host: this.host, type: p.type, tier: TIER[p.type], ctx, start, end: this.s.length, value: p.value }); }
+        else this.s += p;
+      }
+      return this;
+    }
+    text(s) { this.s += s; return this; }
+  }
+  const P = () => {
+    const p = person();
+    return {
+      p, GIVEN: slot('NAME', p.given), SURNAME: slot('NAME', p.surname), FULL: slot('NAME', p.full),
+      GIVEN_UP: slot('NAME', p.given.toUpperCase()), SURNAME_UP: slot('NAME', p.surname.toUpperCase()),
+      given_lower: slot('NAME', p.given.toLowerCase()), SURNAME_ID: slot('NAME', p.surname.replace(/[^\p{L}]/gu, '')),
+      EMAIL: slot('EMAIL', p.email), PHONE: slot('PHONE', phone()), SSN: slot('SSN', ssn()), ITIN: slot('ITIN', itin()),
+      DOB_ISO: slot('DOB', dobISO()), DOB: slot('DOB', dobAny()), DODID: slot('DODID', dodid()), MBI: slot('MBI', mbi()),
+      CARD: slot('CARD', card()), IP: slot('IP', ip()), STREET: slot('ADDR', p.street), CITY: slot('CITY', p.city),
+      STATE: p.state, ZIP: slot('ZIP', p.zip), USERNAME: slot('USERNAME', p.user),
+      PASSPORT: slot('PASSPORT', String(int(100000000, 999999999))), DL: slot('DL', `${pick([...'ABDFGHKLMNPRSTW'])}${pad(int(0, 9999999), 7)}`),
+      MRN: slot('MRN', pad(int(0, 99999999), 8)),
+    };
+  };
+

+    ['D:/tmp/petclinic/src/main/java/org/springframework/samples/petclinic/owner/PetValidator.java', ['java:comment'], 5],

+    if (!existsSync(src)) continue;
+    const lines = readFileSync(src, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');
+    // Insertion points: after a statement or an opening brace inside a body for code; anywhere for data and prose.
+    const code = /\.java$/.test(src);
+    const points = lines.map((l, i) => i).filter((i) => !code || (/[;{]\s*$/.test(lines[i]) && /^\s{4,}|^\t{2,}/.test(lines[i])));
+    const chosen = new Set();
+    while (chosen.size < Math.min(n, points.length)) chosen.add(pick(points));
+    const d = new Doc(`real/${path.basename(path.dirname(src))}__${path.basename(src)}`, 'real');
+    lines.forEach((l, i) => {
+      d.text(l + '\n');
+      if (chosen.has(i)) { const ctx = pick(ctxs); d.add(pick(T[ctx])(P()), ctx); }
+    });
+    docs.push(d);
+  }
+  return docs;

+  const seed = Number(process.argv[2] || 1);

+  rmSync(out, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  const docs = generate(seed);
+  const manifest = [];
+  for (const d of docs) {
+    const f = path.join(out, d.file);
+    mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
+    writeFileSync(f, d.s, 'utf8');
+    manifest.push(...d.spans);
+  }
+  writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'manifest.json'), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 1));
+  const byTier = manifest.reduce((m, s) => ((m[s.tier] = (m[s.tier] || 0) + 1), m), {});
+  console.log(`seed ${seed}: ${docs.length} files, ${manifest.length} planted values ${JSON.stringify(byTier)}`);

+/**
+ * Hand-curated vocabulary for the inverted masker (piishape). Everything here is a word that is safe to show
+ * BECAUSE of what it is in software, not because it is common English — the English list comes from SCOWL and
+ * is built into lexicon.json by build-lexicon.mjs.
+ *
+ * Kept deliberately separate and readable: this is the part of the design a reviewer should be able to argue
+ * with line by line. Anything in JARGON that also appears on a Census or SSA name list (jackson, jenkins, …) is
+ * split out by the build and allowed only where code is being read, never in strings, comments or data.
+ */
+
+const words = (s) => s.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
+
+/** Reserved words and built-ins across the languages a review meets. Revealed in code position anywhere. */
+export const KEYWORDS = new Set(words(`
+  abstract assert boolean break byte case catch char class const continue default do double else enum extends
+  final finally float for goto if implements import instanceof int interface long native new package private
+  protected public return short static strictfp super switch synchronized this throw throws transient try void
+  volatile while true false null var record sealed permits yield module requires exports opens uses provides
+  with to transitive open non
+  async await debugger delete export function in let typeof undefined of get set constructor declare type
+  namespace readonly keyof infer unknown never any as is satisfies from require
+  and def del elif except global lambda nonlocal not or pass raise self cls print len range dict list tuple str
+  isinstance none
+  using internal struct override virtual partial base object decimal string
+  func chan go select defer fallthrough map nil make append cap
+  fun val when companion data inline suspend lateinit
+  insert into values update delete create table drop alter add column index primary key foreign references
+  unique constraint join inner left right outer on order group by having limit offset union all distinct then
+  end exists count sum avg min max varchar integer bigint smallint tinyint numeric timestamp datetime text serial
+  identity cascade commit rollback begin transaction grant revoke view trigger procedure returns declare engine
+  charset collate auto_increment unsigned

+// (quick, 2026-10-27: "quick question 3" reached the bookkeeper as "#xxxxx#… question 3", live 2578 stream test)
+// (bolt, 2026-10-26: a product "bolt" in a CSV reached the agent masked, and it reported "(masked product)", live 2544)
+// (north / south / east / west, 2026-10-26: the region "north" in a CSV row reached the bookkeeper as "#xxxxx#…", live 2540)
+// (row / rows, 2026-10-25: "// 1. Row count" in a script reached the agent as "// 1. #Xxx#… count", live 2528 round B)
+// (cool / nice / fine / lovely / sweet / super, 2026-10-24: the developer's reply "cool" reached the agent as
+// "#xxxx#qw34vhz", and it asked whether the message was sent by accident - chat words the lexicon holds as ambiguous)
+// (button / main / banner / cancel / close / wait / dollar / child / spinner, 2026-10-24: a dev check on prod found
+// "Wait", "Cancel", "Dollar" and the DOM's <button> masked in a page snapshot and AGENTS.md; each is a surname ranked
+// 2800 or lower. NOT page: rank 383, 83k people - like will and young)
+// (pass, 2026-10-23: a review sub-agent's test output "PASS? false" reached it as "#XXXX#? false")
+// (fs / const / YYYY-MM-DD / Unknown / POST / normalised / Click, 2026-10-21: a long live run's agent said its view
+// of its own README showed them as tokens; `require('fs')` reached it as require('#xx#'))
+// (ok / bad / check, 2026-10-14: a test's "CHECK OK" was shown to the agent as "#XXXXX# #XX#", "2 bad rows" as
+// "2 #xxx# rows" - the developer: "whenever you see words that are obviously not names, add them")
+// (show / shows / shown, 2026-10-15: code_boss's own "dsList() shows what there is" reached the agent as
+// "dsList() #xxxxx# what there is" - a word right after a call is judged as code)
+// NOT exempted, although they are as common in prose: will, may, well, long, short, little, good, best, high, low,
+// young, early — each is also a common surname or given name, and the first recall run found "Will Rivers" and
+// "YOUNG" in exports shown in the clear because an earlier version of this list let them through.
+
+/** Month names: a number beside one is part of a date, and a date is treated as a birth date. */
+export const MONTHS = new Set(words(`january february march april may june july august september october november december
+  jan feb mar apr jun jul aug sep sept oct nov dec`));
+
+/** The month names that are nobody's name, shown on their own too. The dictionary leaves them out as proper nouns. */
+export const PROSE_MONTHS = new Set(words(`february october november december`));
+// (2026-10-23: a review sub-agent's "--- December edge case ---" reached it as "--- #Xxxxxxxx# #xxxx# case ---" - the
+// month taken for a name hid the word beside it too. Not january, april, may, june, august, september, march: each is a
+// given name in the name lists, or a surname above the floor - january is both, "String firstName = "January"")
+
+/** Languages and nationalities — proper adjectives, so SCOWL leaves them out, and none of them is a person. */
+export const LANGUAGES = new Set(words(`english french spanish german italian portuguese chinese japanese korean russian
+  arabic hindi bengali urdu dutch swedish norwegian danish finnish latin greek hebrew turkish polish czech slovak
+  hungarian romanian bulgarian ukrainian vietnamese thai indonesian malay filipino tagalog persian farsi swahili
+  american british canadian australian european african asian mexican brazilian indian irish scottish welsh
+  unicode utc gmt est edt cst cdt mst mdt pst pdt`));
+
+/** Standards bodies: the number after one of these is a standard, not an identifier. */
+export const STANDARDS = new Set(words(`iso rfc ieee ansi nist fips sp cve cwe ecma iec itu w3c jsr pep owasp hipaa
+  hl7 icd section chapter article clause part`));
+
+/** Oracle's error-message prefixes: ORA-00942, TNS-12541, PLS-00201, SP2-0734. The code identifies a message, not a
+ *  person (the developer, 2026-10-15: "I want to exempt oracle error codes ORA-01772"). */
+export const ORACLE_ERROR_PREFIXES = new Set(words(`ora tns pls sp2 imp exp kup lrm rman nnl drg prvf prvg crs clsrsc lpx lsx
+  ude oci amd dgm`));
+
+/** Country-code and generic top-level domains, and hostnames that are infrastructure rather than people. */
+export const TLDS = new Set(words(`com org net gov mil edu io dev app co us uk ca de fr jp au info biz int local
+  internal corp lan intra test example invalid localhost`));
+
+/** Units and ordinal suffixes that follow a number. */
+export const UNITS = new Set(words(`ms s sec secs second seconds min mins minute minutes h hr hrs hour hours d day
+  days kb mb gb tb kib mib gib b bytes px pt em rem vh vw deg rad hz khz mhz ghz kbps mbps bps fps dpi st nd rd th
+  x k m g l ml mg kg lb lbs oz ft in cm mm km mi mph kph pct percent rows records items users requests reqs tps rps
+  notes characters chars words lines tokens files entries messages tests times iterations steps attempts retries
+  calls events errors commits pages results queries jobs tasks`));
+// (notes ... tasks, 2026-10-20: "Limit note text to 10000 characters" reached the agent as "#00000# characters" - a
+// count of things, written before the things, is not a ZIP code)
+
+/** Units of measure of two letters or more. Safe anywhere — unlike the one-letter units, which are also initials. */
+export const MEASURE_UNITS = new Set(words(`mg ml dl kg mcg ug ng mmol umol meq iu bpm mmhg cmh2o cm mm km nm um kb mb gb tb
+  ms ns px pt em rem vh vw hz khz mhz ghz lbs oz ft yd mph kph kpa psi rpm dpi fps kbps mbps gbps sec secs hrs mins`));
+
+/** The only e-mail local parts that name a role rather than a person. */
+export const ROLE_LOCALS = new Set(words(`admin administrator support info noreply no-reply donotreply do-not-reply
+  sales help helpdesk contact security webmaster postmaster hostmaster abuse billing team dev devops ops root
+  mail mailer jobs careers press privacy legal feedback hello notifications alerts service services system
+  test tester qa example user users`));
+
+/** Street-type words. Only used by the address context rule: a number, some words, then one of these. */
+export const STREET_SUFFIXES = new Set(words(`street st avenue ave av road rd lane ln drive dr boulevard blvd court
+  ct place pl way parkway pkwy highway hwy circle cir terrace ter trail trl square sq plaza plz loop pike alley
+  aly row crossing xing ridge point pt run path expressway expy freeway fwy turnpike tpke`));
+
+/** Honorifics and titles: next to a masked name they are still just a title. */
+export const TITLES = new Set(words(`mr mrs ms miss mx dr prof sir madam dear hon rev sgt ssg sfc msg sgm csm pfc
+  spc cpl lt capt cpt maj col gen adm cmdr cdr ens pvt amn sra ssgt tsgt msgt smsgt cmsgt po1 po2 po3 cpo scpo
+  mcpo cwo wo1 cw2 cw3 cw4 cw5 officer agent doctor nurse patient member sponsor beneficiary owner customer user
+  mister junior senior jr sr ii iii iv esq phd md rn`));
+
+/** US state and territory abbreviations, for the "City, ST" context rule. */
+export const STATE_CODES = new Set(words(`al ak az ar ca co ct de fl ga hi id il in ia ks ky la me md ma mi mn ms mo
+  mt ne nv nh nj nm ny nc nd oh ok or pa ri sc sd tn tx ut vt va wa wv wi wy dc pr gu vi as mp aa ae ap`));
+
+/** SQL words that, two or more together inside a string, mean the string is SQL — and so is read as code. */
+export const SQL_MARKERS = new Set(words(`select insert update delete create alter drop from where into values set
+  join order group having union table`));
+
+/** Keys whose value is about a person or a place, whatever it looks like. Under one of these nothing is revealed. */
+export const PERSONAL_KEY_RE = /ssn|social|zip|postal|post_?code|phone|mobile|cell|fax|tel\b|dob|\byob\b|birth|born|e_?mail|mail_?address|address|street|city|town|county|first_?name|last_?name|middle_?name|full_?name|sur_?name|given|family|maiden|nick_?name|patient|member|beneficiar|sponsor|employee|customer|contact|owner|author|lat\b|latitude|lon\b|lng|longitude|geo|coord|account|card|routing|iban|mrn|mbi|medicare|npi|edipi|dod_?id|passport|license|licence|itin|\btin\b|vin\b|plate|signature|signed_?by|guarantor|next_?of_?kin|spouse|parent|guardian|emergency/i;
+
+/** Keys whose numeric value is a setting rather than an identifier. */
+export const SETTING_KEY_RE = /port|timeout|size|count|max|min|limit|ttl|interval|delay|retry|retries|width|height|length|version|level|priority|weight|threshold|capacity|pool|batch|page|offset|duration|rate|percent|ratio|scale|precision|year|month|day|hour|minute|second|millis|seconds|index|order|rank|step|columns|rows|depth|attempts|workers|threads/i;
